### Spec (all assets)

- Format: WebP
- Dimensions: 1536×1024 (3:2)
- Target size: 60–70 KB (acceptable 55–75 KB)
- Style: minimal TokBoostly-inspired restraint; NovaLikes cream/orange identity
- Filename: `{service-slug}-{market}-{section-purpose}.webp`
- Alt: page primary keyword once + description

### Page primary keyword pattern

`Buy {Platform} {Metric} {Canada|Australia|USA|UK}`
